914 entries, Skyrim Companion Dialogue Generator to slack-analysis-report. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **Skyrim Companion Dialogue Generator**: Generates dialogue-only conversations or one-liners for a female Skyrim companion with a promiscuous past who is attracted to the player. ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/ConvSkill/english_gpt4_8/skyrim-companion-dialogue-generator) · ★620 · 5 copies)
- **skyrim-1-5-97-mod-design**: 设计、规划与制作上古卷轴5：天际（The Elder Scrolls V: Skyrim / 老滚5 / Skyrim Special Edition）1.5.97 版本（pre-AE 最终 SE 版，SKSE64 2.0.20 生态）的模组。当用户想学习模组设计、规划或制作任务/对话、随从/NPC、物品装备、法术附魔、世界空间/住宅、Papyrus 脚本、SKSE 生态、插件格式（esp/esm/esl、ESL 化、加载顺序与冲突）或打包发布（BSA/SEQ/FOMOD/依赖声明），或询问 Creation Kit、xEdit、UESP Wiki 资料、1.5.97 与 AE(1.6.x) 差异与兼容问题时，务必使用本技能——即使没提“模组”二字（如“给天际加个随从”“老滚5怎么做任务 mod”“esp 想转 esl”“这段 Papyrus 哪儿错了”）。以 UESP Wiki（en.uesp.net / ck.uesp.net）为参考体系，兼顾教学与直接产出。 ([Pra-Moon/skyrim-ai-modding-skills](https://github.com/Pra-Moon/skyrim-ai-modding-skills/tree/HEAD/skyrim-1-5-97-mod-design) · ★1)

- **skyrim-dialog-system**: Pure reference for how Skyrim's (TES5) dialogue, voice, and quest systems work — every record type (DIAL, INFO, QUST, DLBR, DLVW, SCEN, VTYP, IDLE), its binary layout, field meanings, enums, the condition (CTDA) system, the voice-routing pipeline, and a checklist of everything required for dialogue to fire in-engine. Use when authoring, reading, or debugging Skyrim dialogue/quest/voice records, when reasoning about how a topic reaches an NPC, or when you need the exact structure of any of these records. This is a description of vanilla Skyrim only — it contains no conversion/import logic.
